/home/ftk3187/github/PSED/psed_v2/kb/reports/P181.summary.txt

P181: 11 calls (the recipe fill, gemini-3.8-flash, approved at the cost line: est. $0.15, made $0.13); every other step 0 calls.
Every step done; no check failed.

1. ontology.yaml (history round 0, decided_by user, history.csv lines 520-524): the step qualifier has one definition per value, naming the run's
   recipe field (value_definitions; CODEBOOK prints 'step = <value>' rows under the step row); pulse_time 'steps reactant_A and
   reactant_B', purge_time 'steps purge_A and purge_B' (exposure's definition names no step numbers: unchanged); the species rule ends
   'This rule is about symbol records; settings and observations take their species from the text and the run's recipe fields.'
2. form_pass0 version 3: runs carry reactant_A, reactant_B, carrier_gas (species entries) and process_mode, each with <field>_quote,
   null when not stated (simulations / model runs: when the paper states it for the model). export --check 18 / 18 (18: the fields, the
   species values, a value has a quote).
3. Pass-1 prompt: the PASS-0 UNITS line of a run / simulation carries its recipe words (106 of the 191 run / simulation lines of the
   thirteen; yli18's carry none: its fill is null); RULES '- step: by the run's recipe fields (CODEBOOK step values).'; EXAMPLES: two
   P163 records copied (gre23 pulse_time dez reactant_A, mat23 pulse_time h2s reactant_B). No P163 purge_time record has a species, so
   both examples are pulse_time. Diff: the yli18 prefix, P180 vs now.
4. Recipe fill (kb/extract/recipe_fill.py, records pass '0rf'): 11 calls, 458 field rows, every quote found in its element; the
   pass-0 units byte-identical apart from the fields (asserted). hei24, yim22: skipped (no stated model recipe). yli18: every field null:
   the fill's text (method-named sections and the runs' introduced_in elements, s7 and tabI) does not hold s8.p24, the paragraph that
   states the recipe. Reported, not worked around. Compile reads the latest fill line (0pm or 0rf), so process_mode now comes from this
   fill: gor03 thermal (3 runs, null before); the quotes / elements of mat22, art19, zou23, wer21 changed (values the same).
5. The per-case table first (P180 section 5's 61 empty-step settings), then compile.step_fill: 61 / 61 rows equal. Outcomes:
   reactant_A 16, reactant_B 8, purge_A 6, purge_B 5, empty 26 (no species 18; run fields null 8, all yli18 s8.p24). No observation in
   scope. My reading, disclosed: about 'paper' takes all the paper's runs and fills only when every run naming the species agrees.
6. Score vs P179c, no drop: setting 455 -> 460 (gre23 +2, zou23 +2, gao15 +1), every other type equal. T6 2115 -> 2119: 4 rows, all
   step fills (gre23 s9.p4 x2, zou23 s5.p3 x2: records form rule P158 1 dropped before for an empty step). Fact diff: 153 rows: 24 step
   filled (yim20 tab1), 9 new facts with a filled step, 119 process_mode settings (step 4's fill), 1 comparison gone (gor03 'series of
   3 units varying cycle_number': run_2k_L gained an exposure setting with a filled step, so the units' setting vectors now differ).
   binding_regression 798 / 798; audits 1-5 PASS; export --check 18 / 18; cards 0 errors. check.py --dir: 267 problem rows changed,
   mostly process_mode statement repeats (mat22 325 -> 286). yli18 s8.p24: 8 rows still empty (run fields null); yim20 tab1: 20 rows
   now reactant_A / purge_A / reactant_B / purge_B.
7. Qualifier count (read-only): the ontology lists qualifiers for 38 of its 242 entries (entry field qualifiers); quantities without
   a list use the fallback the user gave (species / gas / step / position / level / reference / stat / over / n, for value facts),
   stated per row. 1444 facts carry qualifiers; 445 carry at least one key outside the reference. Keys outside: species 142
   (pulse_time 68, purge_time 29: their entry lists are [step] only), structure_class 99, stage 82, stat 55, mode 48, between 42,
   over 41, n 41, ratio 37, element 33, kind 31, specimen_class 30, level 30, gas 25, position 24, relative_to 23, phase 22, origin 21,

+def recipe_fill(lines):
+    """P179, P181: the latest fill line of the pass-0 units (process_mode fill or recipe fill) as {unit id: {field, field_quote,
+    field_element}} for export.RECIPE_FIELDS (a process_mode fill holds process_mode with quote and element); (line number, fields)."""
+    n = max((i for i, x in enumerate(lines, 1) if x["pass"] in FILL_PASSES), default=None)
+    if n is None:
+        return None, {}
+    out = {}
+    for x in (C.loads_json(lines[n - 1]["response"]).get("recipes") or []):
+        if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("id"):
+            f_ = {}
+            for f in C.X.RECIPE_FIELDS:
+                if f in x:
+                    legacy = f == "process_mode" and f + "_quote" not in x
+                    f_.update({f: x.get(f), f + "_quote": x.get("quote") if legacy else x.get(f + "_quote"), f + "_element": x.get("element") if legacy else x.get(f + "_element")})
+            out[x["id"]] = f_
+    return n, out

+    def step_fill(self, recs):
+        """P181 (user), every form version: the definitional step of a setting or observation of pulse_time, purge_time or exposure whose
+        step is empty, from its run's recipe fields (the recipe fill): its species = the run's reactant_A -> reactant_A (pulse_time,
+        exposure) / purge_A (purge_time); = reactant_B -> reactant_B / purge_B, and for a run of process_mode plasma a pulse_time of its
+        reactant_B -> plasma. The record's run: its about unit (a run or a simulation), a specimen's parent run, a model run's simulation;
+        about 'paper': the paper's runs, when every one naming the species gives it the same role. No species, no run, the run's fields
+        null, a species matching neither, runs disagreeing: the step stays empty (a log line 'a1: step fill ...'). A step set is untouched."""
+        units = {u["id"]: u for u in self.pass0 if isinstance(u, dict) and not u.get("origin")}
+        runs = [u for u in units.values() if u.get("role") in ("run", "simulation")]
+        for r in recs:
+            rec = r["rec"]
+            q = rec.get("qualifiers") if isinstance(rec.get("qualifiers"), dict) else {}
+            if r.get("origin") or rec.get("type") not in ("setting", "observation") or rec.get("quantity") not in C.X.STEP_QUANTITIES or q.get("step"):
+                continue
+            sp, ab = q.get("species") or "", rec.get("about") if isinstance(rec.get("about"), str) else ""
+            u = units.get(ab)
+            cand = runs if ab == "paper" else [] if not u else [u] if u.get("role") in ("run", "simulation") else [units[u["parent"]]] if u.get("parent") in units else []
+            roles = {("A", "") if sp == x.get("reactant_A") else ("B", x.get("process_mode") or "") for x in cand if sp and sp in (x.get("reactant_A"), x.get("reactant_B"))}
+            step, why = "", ("no species" if not sp else f"no run (about {ab or '-'})" if not cand else
+                             "run fields null" if not any(x.get("reactant_A") or x.get("reactant_B") for x in cand) else
+                             "species matches neither field" if not roles else "runs disagree on the species' role" if len(roles) > 1 else "")
+            if not why:
+                half, mode = next(iter(roles))
+                step = (f"purge_{half}" if rec["quantity"] == "purge_time" else
+                        "plasma" if half == "B" and mode == "plasma" and rec["quantity"] == "pulse_time" else f"reactant_{half}")
+                rec["qualifiers"] = {**q, "step": step}
+                why = "the run's recipe field"
+            self.count("a1", f"step fill: {step or 'empty'} ({why})")
+            self.note(f"a1: step fill {r['eid']} {rec['quantity']} {rec.get('value')} species {sp or '-'} about {ab or '-'}: {step or '(empty)'} ({why})")
+        return recs

@@ -0,0 +1,88 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""P181 (user): the recipe fields of each pass-0 run, one call per paper with runs (the P179 process_mode fill's mechanism, process_mode_fill.py):
+reactant_A, reactant_B, carrier_gas (species entries) and process_mode (the ontology's values), each with the quote that states it and the
+element holding it, or null when the text does not state it. Input: the runs of the pass-0 line compile uses (id, name, evidence quote,
+the reactant, step and plasma settings pass 1 gave them or the paper) and the method / experimental text (the sections whose heading names
+an experiment, a method, a procedure or the materials, the sections and elements where a run is introduced). The response is one records
+line of pass RECIPE_FILL_PASS (compile and the pass-1 prompt read the latest fill); nothing else of pass 0 changes (asserted: the units
+without the recipe fields are byte-identical).
+
+    ~/miniconda3/bin/conda run -n psed310 python psed_v2/kb/extract/recipe_fill.py yli18 [--plan]
+"""
+import json
+import sys
+
+import common as C
+import compile as K
+import process_mode_fill as PM
+
+THINKING = {"thinking_level": "LOW"}
+FIELDS = C.X.RECIPE_FIELDS
+DROP = tuple(f + x for f in FIELDS for x in ("", "_quote", "_element"))
+
+
+def job(pid):
+    """(pass, section, prompt, response schema, element ids) or None when the paper has no run."""
+    base = PM.job(pid)                                                          # the runs, their settings and the text, as the P179 fill reads them
+    if base is None:
+        return None
+    _, _, pm_prompt, pm_schema, els = base
+    head, rest = pm_prompt.split("\n\nRECIPES", 1)
+    runs = pm_schema["properties"]["recipes"]["items"]["properties"]["id"]["enum"]
+    species = [i for i, e in K.V.ENTRIES.items() if e.get("type") == "species"]
+    values = list(K.V.CATEGORICAL_VALUES["process_mode"])
+    defs = "; ".join(f"{f}: {K.V.ENTRIES[f]['definition']}" for f in FIELDS)
+    prompt = (
+        "You read the method / experimental text of one ALD paper and give, for each recipe (a deposition run) listed below, its recipe fields: "
+        f"reactant_A, reactant_B, carrier_gas (species ids: {', '.join(species)}) and process_mode (one of {', '.join(values)}). The fields: {defs}. "
+        "A field is stated for a run when the text states it for that run, for a set of runs it belongs to, or for every deposition of the paper; a "
+        "run's own name in the text stating it states it too. reactant_A and reactant_B are the reactants in the order the text gives the cycle "
+        "(the precursor dosed first, then the co-reactant). For each field give the quote copied verbatim from the TEXT (the words that state it) "
+        "and the id of the element holding it. A field the text does not state: the value null, its quote null, its element null. Never infer a "
+        "field from another field, a number or a typical process."
+        "\n\nRECIPES" + rest)
+    item = {"id": {"enum": runs}}
+    for f in FIELDS:
+        item[f] = {"anyOf": [{"enum": species if f != "process_mode" else values}, {"type": "null"}]}
+        item[f + "_quote"] = {"anyOf": [{"type": "string", "minLength": 1}, {"type": "null"}]}
+        item[f + "_element"] = {"anyOf": [{"enum": els}, {"type": "null"}]}
+    schema = {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": ["recipes"],
+              "properties": {"recipes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "required": list(item), "properties": item}}}}
+    return (K.RECIPE_FILL_PASS, "recipe", prompt, schema, els)
+
+
+def main(arg):
+    pid, key = C.resolve(arg)
+    j = job(pid)
+    if j is None:
+        print(f"{key}: no run in pass 0: no call")
+        return 0
+    if "--plan" in sys.argv:
+        C.plan(pid, [j], THINKING)
+        print(f"{key}: prompt {len(j[2])} chars (~{int(len(j[2]) / 3.1)} tokens), {len(j[3]['properties']['recipes']['items']['properties']['id']['enum'])} runs")
+        return 0
+    out, _ = C.run(pid, [j], THINKING)
+    fill = {x["id"]: x for x in C.loads_json(out[0]["response"]).get("recipes") or []}
+    _, _, units = PM.pass0_line(pid)
+    merged = json.loads(json.dumps(units))
+    for u in merged:
+        if u.get("id") in fill:
+            u.update({k: v for k, v in fill[u["id"]].items() if k != "id"})
+    strip = [{k: v for k, v in u.items() if k not in DROP} for u in merged]
+    assert json.dumps(strip, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True) == json.dumps([{k: v for k, v in u.items() if k not in DROP} for u in units], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True), \
+        "pass-0 units changed beyond the recipe fields"
+    text = C.E.texts(pid)
+    for u in units:
+        if u.get("id") in fill:
+            x = fill[u["id"]]
+            cells = []
+            for f in FIELDS:
+                q, t = K.normalize(x.get(f + "_quote") or ""), K.normalize(text.get(x.get(f + "_element") or "", ""))
+                found = "-" if not x.get(f) else ("found" if q and (q in t or K.in_order(q.split(), t)) else "NOT FOUND")
+                cells.append(f"{f} {x.get(f) or 'null'} [{x.get(f + '_element') or '-'}; quote {found}] {x.get(f + '_quote') or ''}")
+            print(f"{key} | {u['id']} | " + " | ".join(cells))
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    sys.exit(C.main(main))
